Checked every named toggle-style toolbar button (move line, bold, italic, heading, color, strikethrough, toggle checkbox) for the same class of bug across their SVG icons, .readiness-btn state styling, and .active glow-ring styling — found no var(--bg)/var(--bg2) reference in any of them; root cause for those not yet identified, more detail requested from Lynn.
```text
  v0.1539 - Three more color-scoping fixes plus a real UX bug found: (1) .note-inline-title-row (the opaque background directly behind the note title text) hardcoded var(--bg) instead of the Note Paper override — same bug class as v0.1537/1538. (2) The paste icon's SVG has two deliberate "cutout" rects styled fill:var(--bg) to look like blank paper on a clipboard; now fill:var(--note-toolbar-bg, var(--bg)) so they blend with the toolbar instead of General UI background. (3) ROOT CAUSE FOUND for "Apply doesn't seem to do anything" in the custom color picker: there was no .custom-color-btn.selected CSS rule at all, and the Apply handler never added a selected indicator to the custom button itself (only to matching PRESET swatches, which a custom hex will never match) — so custom colors were very likely being applied correctly all along, with zero visual confirmation that they had been. Added the missing CSS rule and a shared syncColorRowSelection() helper, wired into both the Apply handler and dialog-open sync, so the active custom slot (if any) now gets the same selection ring presets get. Noted but NOT touched: a pre-existing mismatch where the "Custom Colors" section's checkbox-color custom button maps to customColor while the Color-Coding Palette's preset row for the same visual area maps to checkboxColor — a different setting; flagged for discussion, not silently changed.
  v0.1538 - Fixed two more instances of v0.1536's "hardcoded var(--bg2) instead of the Note Toolbar override" bug: #toolbar-collapse-tab and .toolbar-scroll-arrow now use var(--note-toolbar-bg, var(--bg2)) like the rest of the toolbar. (The note editor's title/name background was already correct as of v0.1537 — it lives in .note-content-area, which is Note Paper, not Note Toolbar.) Still investigating: the reported "Apply in the custom color picker doesn't take effect for most fields" — applySettings() itself verified working correctly in isolation; root cause not yet found, more repro detail requested.
  v0.1537 - Fixed v0.1536's Note Paper color bug: .note-content-area (a child element painting over #note-screen) had its own hardcoded background:var(--bg), completely blocking the new --note-paper-bg override from ever showing. Now uses the same var(--note-paper-bg, var(--bg)) fallback chain as #note-screen itself.
  v0.1536 - Profiles & Note Styles Phase A (build 1 of 5): Note Toolbar and Note Paper now have their OWN colors, independent of General UI. Settings > Colors gains two new subsections (Note Toolbar: bg only; Note Paper: bg+fg), each with a "Match general colors" toggle defaulting ON so this build is a visual no-op until a note style opts out. New settings: lightNoteToolbarBgColor, darkNoteToolbarBgColor, lightNotePaperBg, lightNotePaperFg, darkNotePaperBg, darkNotePaperFg, matchGeneralNoteToolbarColors, matchGeneralNotePaperColors. Implemented via scoped CSS variables (--note-toolbar-bg, --note-paper-bg, --note-paper-fg) with fallback to the existing --bg2/--bg/--fg, so nothing renders differently unless a toggle is turned off.
  v0.1535 - Speech overhaul: fenced code blocks are now skipped entirely (content, not just delimiters) with one paused "code block" announcement instead of reading raw code or garbled content. Emoji and common Western emoticons (:) ;) :( <3 etc.) are now announced by name with a pause before each, using real timed pauses on the on-device Speak button (via a new segment-based speech queue) and comma-based pauses in Copy/Share for speech plain text. Emoji table is curated from Unicode's official Emoji Frequency data (groups 0-4, ~80 entries); anything outside the table gets a generic "emoji" announcement rather than being read as raw Unicode or silently dropped.
  v0.1534 - Copy as plain text (stripForShare) now preserves blank lines exactly as typed instead of collapsing all paragraph spacing to single-spaced; a line only disappears now if it becomes empty after stripping bracket tags, not if it started blank. Speech stripping (stripMarkdownToPlainText, used by Copy for speech / Share for speech / the on-device Speak button) now blanks out a line that is left with nothing but a bare list marker (a lone -, *, or +) after stripping, instead of leaving the bare character for TTS to mispronounce as "semicolon" or similar.
  v0.1533 - Closed a gap in v0.1532's URL-encoding-corruption defense: pasting into a NEW/empty note via iOS's popup Paste menu doesn't always fire our native paste event listener at all (confirmed the app's own old auto-paste-prompt code — already dead/commented out — was not the cause), so that specific path's corrupted text was landing untouched. Added a same-tick safety-net check in the editor's shared input listener: whenever the whole editor value matches the accidental-URL-encoding signature, it is decoded and toasted immediately, regardless of which insertion path bypassed the paste-event patch.
  v0.1532 - ROOT CAUSE FOUND for the %20 paste corruption: it was never in our code — iOS's pasteboard data-detector treats copied text beginning with "Word:" (e.g. a stripped "Items:" heading) as a URI scheme and adds a percent-encoded URL flavor, which iOS popup-menu paste sometimes picks over the plain text. Two defenses added: note-content copies now write explicit text/plain + text/html flavors via ClipboardItem (falls back to writeText), and all Remember paste paths detect the scheme-plus-%20/%0A corruption signature and decode it automatically with a visible toast. Also: System Log dialog text is now selectable (scoped to that dialog only).
  v0.1531 - Diagnostic-only build (continues v0.1530's %20-encoding investigation): copyNoteAsPlainText() and copyNoteForSpeech() now log a checksum/length/head-tail sample of exactly what is written to the clipboard; the paste handler's plain-text branch logs the same for what it reads back. No behavior change. Confirmed via v0.1530 logging that the %20 corruption is NOT caused by encodeURIComponent, the href/link paste branch, or Apple Notes round-tripping — it reproduced with hasHTML=false and clean-looking sample text, so this build adds copy-side logging to compare what was sent vs. what came back.
  v0.1530 - Diagnostic-only build: added debug logging (system log + persistent tap-to-copy toast) to the native paste handler's link-detection branch, to capture the raw clipboard HTML and any <a href> values when an iOS popup-menu paste is suspected of introducing percent-encoded (%20-style) text. No behavior change.
  v0.1529 - Reverted v0.1528's ineffective selection change and instead added overscroll-behavior:contain to the note scroll container to stop the iOS boundary scroll-sticking (first swipe at very top/bottom being eaten by the page rubber-band). Check note now copies its issue list to the clipboard (paste it anywhere to work through the fixes) and flags a checkbox written without its "- " marker.
  v0.1528 - Line numbers: fixed code-block shading bleeding into the number gutter (the code scroll-wrapper background now starts after the gutter). Also addressed the iOS-Safari-only first-tap-before-scroll issue (never occurred in PWA or on Android): text selection is disabled in the viewer while line numbers are on, which removes the iOS selection-anchor that was eating the first scroll touch — turn numbers off to select text.
  v0.1527 - Actually fixed the line-numbers scroll problem (v0.1526's reflow nudge was the wrong cause and made it need two taps — reverted). Real cause: the gutter was per-line PADDING, so a touch in it counted as a caret/selection on that line and swallowed the first scroll. The gutter is now a left MARGIN (outside the line box), with the number in it and pointer-events:none, so touches fall through to scrolling immediately.
  v0.1526 - Fixed the remaining line-numbers scroll problem: on first entry (or right after turning them on) the note would scroll a little and bounce back until you tapped it. The note scroll container now gets a reflow nudge after the layout change, so scrolling works immediately without a priming tap.
  v0.1525 - Fixed a major bug where turning on line numbers blocked vertical scrolling of the note (R&R mode). Cause: the gutter was added as padding on .note-viewer, whose content then overflowed its overflow-x:hidden box and trapped touch scrolling on iOS. The gutter is now indented per-line inside the existing box, so scrolling works normally.
  v0.1524 - Check note's "Show line numbers" button now also offered from Typing mode (when R&R is an allowed mode for the note): a comment above the button explains numbers show in R&R, and the button switches to R&R and turns numbers on in one tap. If R&R is disallowed entirely for the note, no button is shown.
  v0.1523 - New "Show line numbers" (More menu, R&R mode; \u2713 pattern): numbers each raw line in a left gutter via a CSS counter across .viewer-line (no JS bookkeeping needed \u2014 one div per raw line already). Check note's results dialog gains a one-tap "Show line numbers" button when they're off, so the path from an issue's line number to finding it on screen is one tap.
  v0.1522 - Check note's malformed-checkbox rule rewritten as self-contained syntax validation (the previous rule leaned on isCheckboxRaw, which is a marker-line classifier assigned late — it flagged valid checkboxes before a note opened and could never flag invalid ones after); also validates checkbox color-suffix names. Corrected isCheckboxRaw's stale "replaced in init" comment. Deploy this instead of v0.1521.
  v0.1521 - New defined color {white} (#FFFFFF) added to every color list, render map, checkbox palette, CSS variables, and the color picker. New "Check note" in the More menu: lints the note for unclosed highlights/bold/strikethrough, unknown or unbalanced {color} tags, malformed or orphaned #hex: highlight colors, malformed checkbox markers, and unclosed code fences.
  v0.1520 - All 27 note-viewer render sites consolidated onto renderViewer() (stamps now attached on every render path). Audit identified the 2026-07-05 data-loss trigger: the smart-spacing fixup re-rendered mid-move via a bypass path, wiping the stamps the next move commit needed — closed structurally by this consolidation, on top of v0.1516's abort guard.
  v0.1519 - Note snapshot safety net: content is snapshotted (per-note ring of 5, quota-capped) before Move lines, Cut, Uncheck all, Edit Paragraph save, Paste, and recursive check/uncheck of sub-items; new More menu item "Restore note backup" restores the latest snapshot (reversibly — the current state is snapshotted first).
  v0.1518 - REAL fix for "Color-code lines by tag": the bars render 5px left of each line and v0.1495's overflow-x:hidden on .note-viewer had been clipping them invisible; the viewer's clip box now extends 8px left (padding/negative-margin pair, text position unchanged). Corrects v0.1517: its "restored missing toggles" diagnosis was wrong (they already existed); its duplicate toggle lines are removed. Do not deploy v0.1517.
  v0.1517 - Restored "Color-code lines by tag" and "Color-code tag pills by tag" (their body-class toggles had vanished from applySettings while the CSS survived). Applying a text color inside a hex-colored highlight now nests correctly after the #hex: prefix; toggling a colored highlight off no longer leaves the orphaned #hex: as visible text.
  v0.1516 - CRITICAL data-loss fix: Move lines could silently rewrite the whole note from rendered text (stripping ==highlights==, # headings, bold, etc.) whenever line stamps were missing; the commit now aborts loudly instead and never touches rendered text. Stamping consolidated into shared stampRawLinesFromContent(); move commits and single-note export format choices are now debug-logged.
  v0.1515 - Export all as ZIP: notes become .md/.txt files with folders as directories and Remember-only syntax stripped to portable markdown (hex highlight prefixes, {color} tags, checkbox :color extensions, app button tokens); non-empty scratch pad pages included under scratch-pad/; debug log excluded. Dependency-free stored-entry ZIP writer.
  v0.1514 - Highlight color prefixes (==#hex:text==) no longer leak into note-list previews, auto-generated note titles, or plain-text copy/share/speech output — three drifting copies of highlight-unwrapping consolidated into one shared unwrapHighlightsInline() helper.
  v0.1513 - Added "View system log" to Settings > Debugging: shows the always-on ring log (newest first, scrollable) with a Copy button — completing the loop so critical events are readable on-device even when normal debug logging was off.
  v0.1512 - Scratch pad gained session-only per-page undo/redo (two FABs next to Copy; one undo per typing burst). Long-press (or right-click) the highlight toolbar button to pick from the Settings highlight palette; quick taps then bake that color into each new highlight (==#hex:==) so multiple priority colors coexist — "D" swatch returns to the default plain highlight.
  v0.1511 - Fixed scratch pad showing stale (pre-import) text and the page-curl then overwriting freshly imported content — the "page 3 didn't import" bug. Fixed intermittent dead up/down arrows in toolbar reorder: movement now swaps visual rows (shared getToolbarVisualUnits), never raw order entries that render no row; also scroll-over-arrow no longer triggers a move, and each arrow tap is debug-logged.
  v0.1510 - Debug log gains date header lines on day change / session start (entries were time-only and undatable). Scratch pad's three pages now included in JSON export and restored on import (only into empty local pages). saveSettings/saveThemes now fail loudly like saveNotes.
  v0.1509 - Fixed sample notes failing to install on fresh storage: the hidden debug-log note (created by loadState's own diagnostics) counted as an existing note and blocked createInitialNotes — the gate now counts only real notes, plus treats an absent notes key at load start as a fresh install. Census logs now show real vs total note counts.
  v0.1508 - Storage safety: per-deployment localStorage namespacing (stable/dev isolated; main path keeps existing data); always-on 300-entry system log surviving the debug setting; daily quota-aware notes+folders backup (skipped when loss detected); JSON export now includes folders and import rebuilds folder structure.
  v0.1507 - Delete guard now dismisses the keyboard before showing (ends the offscreen-untappable-dialog trap). Select lines button no longer stays lit after backgrounding the app (duplicate visibilitychange handlers consolidated). Skipping or tapping outside a Help-launched tour returns to Help; finishing it still exits.
  v0.1506 - Alpha diagnostics for missing-notes investigation: debug logging force-re-enables on any version change; startup storage census logged; note census written on every save so any notes missing at next load are detected and NAMED in the debug log; corrupt remember_notes is preserved to a backup key instead of being silently overwritten; saveNotes failures now warn loudly instead of being uncaught.
  v0.1505 - Tag autocomplete popup no longer opens from programmatic text changes (only real typing of @) — fixes it appearing after inserting a tag via the Insert Tags dialog. Confirm dialogs now auto-scroll themselves into view when the keyboard has panned the page (best effort, helps most in webviews).
  v0.1504 - Insert Tags dialog: preview auto-sizes up to 5 lines and truly scrolls to the insertion point (mirror-div measurement; old newline-count never worked on soft-wrapped paragraphs); middle tag section capped at ~3 rows with a 1-row minimum; Done row tightened. Tag insert now marks Edit Paragraph dialog dirty. Delete guard position also compensates for page layout scroll (deep-in-note keyboard pan).
  v0.1503 - Insert Tags dialog: paragraph preview grew to ~6 lines; tags from other notes moved to their own bottom section capped at ~2 rows with vertical-only scrolling.
  v0.1502 - Cut keeps Select-lines mode on for repeated cuts. Delete guard: clearer wording (go ahead / undo), stays visible when the keyboard has panned the page, and no longer false-alarms on Edit Paragraph save. Collapsed lower toolbar stays collapsed while typing.
  v0.1501 - Tag autocomplete popup: shows up to 16 tags (was 8, same height, scrolls) and gained an always-visible X close button. Added this changelog.
  v0.1500 - Safe-area insets for iPhone landscape: toolbars, note content, notes list, home header, and quiet FAB now clear the camera pill and rounded corners.
  v0.1499 - Consolidated note-item and folder-item long-press into one shared attachLongPress() helper (behavior unchanged).
  v0.1498 - Restored pinch-zoom in the note editor (touch-action pan-y -> pan-y pinch-zoom) and stopped two-finger holds from opening note/folder context menus on the home screen.
  v0.1497 - Fixed pinch-zoom making the note editor toolbars fill the screen: pinToolbars() no longer glues toolbars to the visual viewport while zoomed (scale > 1).
  v0.1496 - iOS horizontal-scroll fix: JS guard snaps scrollLeft back to 0 on note containers (WebKit caret-drag autoscroll ignores touch-action), with burst-style debug logging of the culprit container.
```
